● Je classe ce projet comme architectural, avec trois volets : évolution du
  plugin de gouvernance UI pour un 3ᵉ framework, installation de Claude
  Code/Codex, et rollout Svelte (~29 composants). Avant de proposer une
  approche, je vais poser quelques questions, une à la fois. · summarized

● Dernier point à trancher avant le design : l'extraction API Svelte pour la
  doc. Le docgen actuel utilise react-docgen-typescript pour React et un
  programme TS + @angular/compiler pour Angular.

● User answered Claude's questions:
  ⎿  · Comment extraire l'API Svelte pour apps/doc (frameworks.svelte dans les 
     JSON) ? → Extracteur TS maison sur le <script lang="ts"> (Recommended)

● Voici le design, en deux sections. Dis-moi si la première te va avant que je
  passe à la suite.

  Section 1 — Plugin + installation (Phase A)

  Principe : un seul endroit par règle (comme evolve-governance l'exige). Svelte
  devient un adaptateur de plein droit, React reste la source par défaut.

  Nouveau
  - skills/sync-svelte-component/SKILL.md (+ agents/openai.yaml) — miroir de
    sync-angular-component avec les nuances Svelte 5 :
    - contrat → $props() typé par une interface SvelteXxxProps, defaults dans la
      destructuration ; onXChange conservés (callback props, pas d'events DOM),
      value contrôlable exposé aussi en $bindable() — la primitive d'état
      contrôlable du core reste l'autorité (bind ≠ controlled) ;
    - children/slots → snippets (children, Snippet<[...]>), pas de <slot> legacy
      ;
    - DOM/animation → $effect + contrôleurs @udixio/core/dom, destroy dans le
      cleanup ; jamais onMount pour ce qui dépend de props réactives ;
    - forme de livraison : le pendant de « directive Angular » est une action
      (use:xxx) ou un attachment ({@attach}) quand le comportement s'attache à
      un élément du consommateur ; store/fonction quand pas d'hôte. Même règle
      FORM-* bloquante.
  - references/svelte-conventions.md (governance) et
    references/svelte-conventions.md (usage) — nuances citées par les audits
    sans les restater.

  Généralisé (React + Angular + Svelte)
  - repository-map.md : lignes Svelte
    (packages/ui-svelte/src/lib/<component>/Xxx.svelte, tests colocalisés
    xxx.spec.ts, examples apps/doc/src/examples/svelte/), naming map Svelte.
  - quality-gates.md : gates Svelte (nx test/build/lint ui-svelte,
    svelte-check), dimension « chaque adaptateur disponible » au lieu de « les
    deux ».
  - audit-parity, audit-multiframework, audit-framework-quality,
    audit-accessibility, audit-documentation, audit-component (ajout de l'étape
    Svelte dans l'orchestration), create-material-component (slice React +
    Angular + Svelte), audit-public-api : formulations « adaptateur cible » au
    lieu d'« Angular ».
  - Scripts : component_inventory.py découvre Svelte ; validate_api_docs.py
    accepte svelte comme framework ; tests pytest mis à jour (fixtures
    pass/fail).
  - Manifests Claude/Codex, README, marketplace : keywords svelte, version
    0.4.0. udixio-ui-usage : descriptions et références couvrent
    @udixio/ui-svelte.

  Installation
  - Claude Code : marketplace déjà déclarée dans .claude/settings.json → claude 
    plugin validate puis marketplace update + install/réinstall, vérif claude 
    plugin list.
  - Codex : marketplace udixio-ui-local déjà connue → bump +codex.<timestamp>
    (cachebuster), codex plugin add udixio-ui-governance@udixio-ui-local (+
    udixio-ui-usage qui n'y est pas encore), vérif codex plugin list.

  Test : pytest des scripts, claude plugin validate, et forward-test = le pilote
  Button de la phase B.

  Ça te convient ?

✻ Sautéed for 2m 22s · done vendredi 18:18

❯ Oui                                                                           

● Section 2 — Infra Svelte + pilote (Phase B) et vagues (Phase C)

  Package packages/ui-svelte (@udixio/ui-svelte)
  - Svelte 5 (runes), TS, build par @sveltejs/package → dist/ avec .svelte +
    .d.ts (les .svelte sont livrés en source, le consommateur compile — standard
    des libs Svelte). exports : . (barrel index.ts) ; peer svelte ^5,
    @udixio/core.
  - Layout : src/lib/<component>/Xxx.svelte, xxx.spec.ts colocalisé,
    src/lib/utils/ pour createStyle-équivalent (rune $derived sur xxxStyle) et
    la primitive d'état contrôlable branchée sur @udixio/core.
  - Tests : vitest + @testing-library/svelte + jsdom (même stack que React, pas
    de jest), vitest-axe pour a11y. Lint : eslint-plugin-svelte. Typecheck :
    svelte-check.
  - Nx : project.json avec build, test, lint, typecheck ; ajout dans
    vitest.workspace.ts ; release aligné sur ui-angular.

  Docs apps/doc
  - @astrojs/svelte ; examples src/examples/svelte/<component>-<case>.svelte
    importés en direct par les MDX comme React/Angular.
  - exampleFrameworkStore : 'svelte' ajouté ; sélecteur, Code/CodePreview,
    api.astro rendent le 3ᵉ framework uniquement quand le payload existe.
  - docgen.js : extracteur Svelte (svelte/compiler parse → script → programme TS
    partagé) produisant frameworks.svelte avec props (defaults, $bindable
    marqué), callbacks, snippets ; schéma versionné ; validate_api_docs.py en
    phase.

  Pilote : Button via udixio-ui-governance:sync-svelte-component (donc
  inventaire → audit API → parité → tests → example → docgen → audit-parity 
  validate). Le pilote sert de forward-test du plugin ; s'il révèle un défaut de
  skill, je corrige le plugin avant la phase C.

  Phase C — vagues (chaque vague = commits séparés, audit-parity en validate par
  composant) :
  1. Simples : Badge, Divider, StateLayer, Card, IconButton, Fab,
     ProgressIndicator, Tooltip
  2. Formulaires : Checkbox, Switch, Slider, TextField, Chip/Chips, Search
  3. Navigation/overlays : AnchorPositioner, Menu(+Item/Group/Headline),
     ContextMenu, FabMenu, Snackbar, SideSheet, NavigationRail(+Item/Section)
  4. Complexes : Tabs (Tab/TabGroup/TabPanel(s)), Carousel(+Item), DatePicker

  Hors périmètre : @udixio/mcp (déprécié), rétro-compat Svelte 4, migration des
  composants React/Angular existants.

  Process : la spec va dans
  docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-18-svelte-adapter-design.md, puis un plan par
  phase (A, B, C-vagues) via writing-plans, exécuté en séquence sur feat/svelte.

  OK pour cette section ?

        1 +# Svelte adapter — design                                     
        2 +                                                              
        3 +Date: 2026-09-18 · Branch: `feat/svelte`                      
        4 +                                                              
        5 +## Goal                                                       
        6 +                                                              
        7 +Add Svelte as a third first-class adapter of Udixio UI (`@udix
          +io/ui-svelte`), governed by the same                          
        8 +plugin, standards and quality gates as React and Angular, and 
          +roll it out to every existing                                 
        9 +component. React remains the default product source; core stay
          +s framework-agnostic.                                         
       10 +                                                              
       11 +## Non-goals                                                  
       12 +                                                              
       13 +- `@udixio/mcp` (deprecated, untouched).                      
       14 +- Svelte 4 / legacy syntax compatibility (`export let`, `<slot
          +>`).                                                          
       15 +- Any change to the existing React and Angular component behav
          +ior beyond defects that an audit                              
       16 +  proves and that block a faithful Svelte port.               
       17 +                                                              
       18 +## Decisions                                                  
       19 +                                                              
       20 +| Question | Decision |                                       
       21 +| --- | --- |                                                 
       22 +| Svelte target | Svelte 5 runes only: `$props()`, `$state`, `
          +$derived`, `$effect`, `$bindable`, snippets |                 
       23 +| Package | `packages/ui-svelte`, published as `@udixio/ui-sve
          +lte`, built by `@sveltejs/package` (ships `.svelte` sources + 
          +`.d.ts`) |                                                    
       24 +| Tests | vitest + `@testing-library/svelte` + jsdom + `vitest
          +-axe`; specs colocated `packages/ui-svelte/src/lib/<component>
          +/<component>.spec.ts` |                                       
       25 +| Lint / typecheck | `eslint-plugin-svelte`, `svelte-check` | 
       26 +| Docs | `@astrojs/svelte`; examples `apps/doc/src/examples/sv
          +elte/<component>-<case>.svelte` imported directly by MDX |    
       27 +| Framework preference | `ExampleFramework = 'react' \| 'angul
          +ar' \| 'svelte'`; pages render Svelte only when a payload/exam
          +ple exists |                                                  
       28 +| API extraction | Own extractor in `apps/doc/scripts/docgen.j
          +s`: `svelte/compiler` `parse` isolates `<script lang="ts">`, t
          +he shared TS program reads the `Svelte<Xxx>Props` interface, `
          +$props()` destructuring defaults, `$bindable()` markers and TS
          +Doc → `frameworks.svelte` |                                   
       29 +| Plugin shape | Dedicated `sync-svelte-component` skill; ever
          +y `audit-*`, `create-material-component`, references and scrip
          +ts generalized to N adapters |                                
       30 +| Rollout | Phase A plugin + install → Phase B infra + Button 
          +pilot → Phase C waves |                                       
       31 +                                                              
       32 +## Svelte adapter conventions (source of truth for the plugin 
          +references)                                                   
       33 +                                                              
       34 +- **Contract**: `packages/ui-svelte/src/lib/<component>/Xxx.sv
          +elte` declares                                                
       35 +  `interface SvelteXxxProps extends XxxProps { … }` and reads 
          +it through `let { … } = $props()`.                            
       36 +  Defaults live in the destructuring only. No hollow prop: eve
          +ry declared prop is wired, as in                              
       37 +  `docs/component-authoring.md`.                              
       38 +- **Events**: shared contract callbacks keep their names (`onV
          +alueChange`) as callback props.                               
       39 +  Svelte 5 lowercased DOM handlers (`onclick`) are forwarded t
          +o the host element via rest props;                            
       40 +  they are not the component API.

❯ tout les differents plans sont essentiel à l'implementation pour ces tâches   
  ci ?                                                                          

● Non, pas tous. Franchement :

  - Phase A (plugin) : un plan écrit n'apporte presque rien — c'est de l'édition
    de markdown/scripts guidée par la spec, et le skill evolve-governance du
    plugin lui-même décrit déjà la procédure. Je peux l'exécuter directement.
  - Phase B (infra + pilote) : c'est la seule où un plan a de la valeur — ordre
    des dépendances (package → vitest workspace → Astro → store → docgen →
    validateur), et des choix techniques qui peuvent casser (svelte-package dans
    Nx, @astrojs/svelte avec React + Angular déjà présents, extracteur docgen).
    Un plan court y sert de checklist.
  - Phase C (vagues) : aucun plan — c'est le plugin qui est la procédure
    (sync-svelte-component composant par composant), et la spec fixe déjà
    l'ordre des vagues.

  Je propose donc : j'exécute la phase A maintenant sans plan, j'écris un plan
  compact pour la B uniquement, puis j'enchaîne la C avec le plugin. Ça te va ?

      43

       1 +# Angular adapter conventions                                  
       2 +                                                               
       3 +What an Angular adapter looks like when it is idiomatic. [sync-
         +angular-component](../skills/sync-angular-component/SKILL.md)  
       4 +owns the conversion procedure; the audit skills cite this page 
         +instead of restating it.                                       
       5 +                                                               
       6 +## Shape                                                       
       7 +                                                               
       8 +- Standalone component with `OnPush`, or an attribute directive
         + when the primary behavior attaches                            
       9 +  to an element the consumer already owns. A service fits behav
         +ior with no host; a pipe fits a pure                           
      10 +  transformation. Choosing a shape that differs from React requ
         +ires an accepted `FORM-*` finding.                             
      11 +- Selector prefix: read it from neighboring components; never a
         +ssume it. A directive's inputs are                             
      12 +  prefixed with the directive name (`udxProgressIndicatorValue`
         +) because the host is not its own.                             
      13 +                                                               
      14 +## Binding idiom                                               
      15 +                                                               
      16 +- One typed `input()` per contract prop, holding the real value
         +; `output()` per callback, without                             
      17 +  React's `on` prefix (`onValueChange` → `valueChange`).       
      18 +- Content projection through `<ng-content>` or typed templates;
         + never a React-shaped `children`                               
      19 +  input.                                                       
      20 +- Signals, `computed`, and the repository's controllable-state 
         +primitive own reactive state.                                  
      21 +- `afterRenderEffect` connects `@udixio/core/dom` controllers; 
         +every controller is destroyed.                                 
      22 +- A directive injects its own `ElementRef`; a component queries
         + its own view. An input that only                              
      23 +  carries a `ref` or `targetRef` is a transliterated mechanism.
      24 +                                                               
      25 +## Host element                                                
      26 +                                                               
      27 +The component host is a real element: account for it in semanti
         +cs, layout, CSS inheritance, border                            
      28 +radius, clipping, and event targeting, with tests.

       1 +# Svelte adapter conventions                                   
       2 +                                                               
       3 +What a Svelte adapter looks like when it is idiomatic. [sync-sv
         +elte-component](../skills/sync-svelte-component/SKILL.md)      
       4 +owns the conversion procedure; the audit skills cite this page 
         +instead of restating it.                                       
       5 +                                                               
       6 +Target: **Svelte 5, runes only**. `export let`, `$:`, `<slot>`,
         + `createEventDispatcher`, and                                  
       7 +`on:event` are legacy syntax and are defects in this repository
         +, not stylistic choices.                                       
       8 +                                                               
       9 +## Shape                                                       
      10 +                                                               
      11 +- A `.svelte` component when the component owns every element i
         +t needs.                                                       
      12 +- An **action** (`use:x`) — or an attachment (`{@attach x()}`) 
         +when the behavior must react to                                
      13 +  props of the consumer's element — when the primary behavior a
         +ttaches to an element the consumer                             
      14 +  already owns. This is the counterpart of an Angular directive
         +: the action receives the host                                 
      15 +  `HTMLElement` itself and returns `update`/`destroy`. A plain 
         +function or store fits behavior with                           
      16 +  no host. Choosing a shape that differs from React requires an
         + accepted `FORM-*` finding.                                    
      17 +- A Svelte component has **no host element**. The template's ro
         +ot element is the semantic element;                            
      18 +  anything React puts on its root element goes there.          
      19 +                                                               
      20 +## Contract                                                    
      21 +                                                               
      22 +- `interface Svelte<Xxx>Props extends <Xxx>Props` in `<script l
         +ang="ts">`, declared with                                      
      23 +  `let { … }: Svelte<Xxx>Props = $props()`. It is the public AP
         +I and the docgen source: TSDoc lives on                        
      24 +  its members, defaults live in the destructuring only.        
      25 +- Rest props (`...rest`) are typed with the native element's at
         +tributes                                                       
      26 +  (`HTMLButtonAttributes`, …) and spread on the root element, s
         +o consumers keep `class`, `onclick`,                           
      27 +  `aria-*`, and data attributes. Resolved states and internal p
         +rops never reach the DOM.                                      
      28 +- Callback props keep the contract names: `onValueChange`, not 
         +`onvaluechange`, not a                                         
      29 +  `CustomEvent`. The lowercased form is Svelte's DOM-event idio
         +m and stays reserved for native                                
      30 +  events forwarded through rest props.                         
      31 +- A controllable value is additionally declared `$bindable()` s
         +o `bind:value` works. Binding is an                            
      32 +  ergonomic surface, not a second state engine: the core contro
         +llable-state primitive still decides                           
      33 +  every transition, blocked states still block, and `onValueCha
         +nge` still fires. Controlled usage                             
      34 +  (owner passes `value` and handles `onValueChange` without bin
         +ding) must keep working.                                       
      35 +- Content: `children?: Snippet`, and `Snippet<[state]>` for wha
         +t React expresses as a render prop.                            
      36 +  Name slots after the contract concept (`leadingIcon`), not af
         +ter their position.                                            
      37 +- `class` is the consumer-facing name of what React calls `clas
         +sName`; it accepts the same                                    
      38 +  `string | ClassNameComponent<XxxInterface>` union and is forw
         +arded to the core style function                               
      39 +  unchanged.                                                   
      40 +

       4 +---                                                            
       5 +                                                               
       6 +# Synchronize Svelte from React                                
       7 +                                                               
       8 +## Resolve the source                                          
       9 +                                                               
      10 +1. Read [the repository map](../../references/repository-map.md
         +),                                                             
      11 +   [svelte-conventions.md](../../references/svelte-conventions.
         +md), current authoring/behavior docs,

      13 +2. Apply [the public API standard](../../references/public-api-
         +standard.md) to every source prop,                             
      14 +   callback, default, and type before conversion. React is not 
         +presumed correct.                                              
      15 +3. If a contract is negative, implementation-shaped, ambiguous,

         +ract. The deciding question is                                 
      21 +   attachment, not rendering: does the component's primary beha
         +vior **attach** to an element the

      26 +   with no host. When the answer is not the source adapter's sh

         + its callback, and route both                                  
      38 +  through the core controllable-state primitive: `bind:` never 
         +bypasses a blocked transition or                               
      39 +  silences `onXChange`.                                        
      40 +- Map `children` and render props to snippets typed with the st
         +ate they receive. Never emit

     -0)
       1 +# Svelte conventions for @udixio/ui-svelte                     
       2 +                                                               
       3 +## Setup                                                       
       4 +                                                               
       5 +- Requires `svelte` `^5` — the components are written with rune
         +s and ship as `.svelte` sources plus `.d.ts` (standard Svelte l
         +ibrary packaging), so the consumer's own Vite/SvelteKit build c
         +ompiles them. There is no Svelte 4 build.                      
       6 +- Install `@udixio/theme` and `@udixio/tailwind` alongside `@ud
         +ixio/ui-svelte` (`npm install @udixio/ui-svelte @udixio/theme @
         +udixio/tailwind`). Like Angular — and unlike React — `@udixio/u
         +i-svelte` does **not** re-export the theme engine: `defineConfi
         +g` comes from `@udixio/theme`, and the theme CSS is produced at
         + build time by the `@udixio/theme` Vite plugin wired into `vite
         +.config`. Add a Tailwind `@source` directive pointing at `node_
         +modules/@udixio/ui-svelte` so Tailwind picks up the library's u
         +tility classes. See `https://ui.udixio.fr/get-started/svelte.md
         +` for the full walkthrough; if that page does not exist yet for
         + the installed version, follow the Angular page's build-plugin 
         +steps and say so.                                              
       7 +- There is no Svelte `ThemeProvider` — components need no provi
         +der. Runtime color changes go through `@udixio/theme`'s `loader
         +()` API, as in Angular.                                        
       8 +                                                               
       9 +## Props                                                       
      10 +                                                               
      11 +- The public props type is exported as `Svelte<Component>Props`
         + (e.g. `SvelteButtonProps`) next to the component; read it from
         + the shipped `.d.ts` rather than the `.svelte` file's script bl
         +ock when both exist.                                           
      12 +- `class` is the customization prop (what React calls `classNam
         +e`). It accepts a plain string or a `ClassNameComponent<XInterf
         +ace>` map for state-aware classes.                             
      13 +- Callback props keep the React names: `onValueChange`, `onPres
         +sedChange`, `onCheckedChange`… They are plain callback props, *
         +*not** Svelte custom events — there is no `on:valuechange`, no 
         +`event.detail`.                                                
      14 +- Controlled state: a value prop (`checked`, `value`, `pressed`
         +) is also `$bindable`, so `bind:checked` is the idiomatic way t
         +o keep it in sync; `default*` props initialize uncontrolled use
         +. Pass the value prop plus its `on*Change` callback only when y
         +ou need to intercept or reject a change. Never combine `bind:` 
         +with the same prop's `default*` counterpart on one instance.   
      15 +- Boolean props are plain booleans: `disabled={true}` or the sh
         +orthand `disabled` — there is no attribute coercion beyond Svel
         +te's own.                                                      
      16 +                                                               
      17 +## Content and DOM                                             
      18 +                                                               
      19 +- Content goes through snippets: `children` for the default slo
         +t, named snippets (`leadingIcon`, …) for the others — `{#snippe
         +t leadingIcon()}…{/snippet}` inside the component tag. Legacy `
         +<slot>` / `slot="…"` syntax is not supported.                  
      20 +- Native attributes and DOM handlers (`aria-*`, `data-*`, `oncl
         +ick`, `onkeydown`) are forwarded to the component's root elemen
         +t through rest props. Use Svelte 5's lowercased handler form fo
         +r those (`onclick`), and the camelCase callback form for the co
         +mponent's own callbacks (`onPressedChange`).                   
      21 +- To reach the rendered element, use `bind:this` on the compone
         +nt only where its props type declares a bindable element ref; o
         +therwise wrap it and query the wrapper.                        
      22 +- Some components are delivered as actions (`use:tooltip={…}`) 
         +rather than components when their job is to attach behavior to 
         +an element you own — the docs page states the shape. Actions ar
         +e imported from the same package barrel.

       1 +import type {                                                  
       2 +  ClassNameComponent,

       7 +                                                               
       8 +/**                                                            
       9 + * Paints the Material 3 state layer over its trigger, and driv
         +es the press                                                   
      10 + * ripple.                                                     
      11 + *                                                             
      12 + * @status beta                                                
      13 + * @category Interaction                                       
      14 + * @devx                                                       
      15 + * - Building block used by every interactive component (`Butto
         +n`, `Chip`, `Switch`, ...); it is                              
      16 + *   rendered inside the element it decorates, never standalone
         +.                                                              
      17 + * - `colorName` is a theme token without the `--color-` prefix
         +, and it is the                                                
      18 + *   content colour of the surface being decorated -- `on-prima
         +ry` on a filled                                                
      19 + *   button, `on-primary-container` once that button is repaint
         +ed onto a                                                      
      20 + *   container. An unknown token degrades to `on-surface`.     
      21 + * - `stateClassName` selects the Tailwind utility driving the 
         +CSS states. The                                                
      22 + *   `state-ripple-group-[name]` form pairs with a `group/name`
         + class on the                                                  
      23 + *   trigger, and the layer attaches its ripple to that same el
         +ement, so the                                                  
      24 + *   CSS states and the JavaScript gesture always agree on what
         + the trigger is.                                               
      25 + * - `state-layer` (the non-group form) is CSS-only: no ripple 
         +is wired.                                                      
      26 + * @a11y                                                       
      27 + * - Decorative only: the layer carries `aria-hidden` and takes
         + no pointer                                                    
      28 + *   events, so it never reaches the accessibility tree nor int
         +ercepts input.                                                 
      29 + * - The press ripple honours the reduced-motion preference thr
         +ough the shared                                                
      30 + *   controller.                                               
      31 + * @limitations                                                
      32 + * - Must be rendered inside the element carrying the named Tai
         +lwind group,                                                   
      33 + *   which is the trigger it attaches to.                      
      34 + * - `colorName` is typed as `string`: the valid tokens are not
         + visible from                                                  
      35 + *   here, so a typo degrades to `on-surface` instead of failin
         +g to compile.                                                  
      36 + * - `classes` takes the state-aware function form, but it reso
         +lves no                                                        
      37 + *   interaction states -- hover, focus and press live in the T
         +ailwind                                                        
      38 + *   utilities, not in JavaScript.                             
      39 + */

      17 +`noUnusedLocals` and reference `svelteHTML` outside its own typ

         +ins the real type gate. It also                                
      20 +drops the `development` export condition so `@udixio/core` is r
         +esolved through its built typings,

✻ Sautéed for 54m 29s · done 8:02

❯ je n'ai pas trop compris                                                      

● Pas de souci, je reprends plus simplement. La question porte sur comment un 
  développeur qui utilise @udixio/ui-svelte contrôle l'état d'un composant (par
  exemple pressed sur un bouton toggle).

  En React, il y a deux modes :

  // non contrôlé : le bouton gère son état, tu es juste prévenu
  <Button toggleable defaultPressed onPressedChange={console.log} />

  // contrôlé : c'est TOI qui décides ; si tu ne mets pas à jour `pressed`, rien
  ne bouge
  <Button toggleable pressed={pressed} onPressedChange={(next) => { if (ok)
  setPressed(next); }} />

  En Svelte 5, le mode non contrôlé est identique. Pour le mode contrôlé, il y a
  un obstacle technique : un composant Svelte ne peut pas savoir si le parent a
  écrit pressed={x} (valeur simple) ou bind:pressed={x} (liaison). Il faut donc
  choisir une convention. Celle que j'ai implémentée :

  svelte
  <!-- cas courant : liaison, ça marche tout seul -->
  <Button toggleable bind:pressed />

  <!-- cas « je veux pouvoir refuser » : liaison avec fonctions (Svelte ≥ 5.9)
  -->
  <Button toggleable bind:pressed={() => pressed, (next) => { if (ok) pressed =
  next; }} />

  Le composant, quand un clic est accepté, appelle onPressedChange(next) et
  assigne pressed = next. Avec bind: classique, ta variable suit. Avec la
  liaison à fonctions, c'est ton setter qui décide : si tu n'assignes pas, le
  bouton reste dans son état. C'est l'idiome officiel de Svelte 5 pour ce cas,
  et ça donne le même comportement que React.

  Ce qui change par rapport à ce qu'on avait écrit dans la spec : écrire
  pressed={false} sans bind: et sans rien faire dans le callback ne « bloque »
  pas le bouton comme en React — Svelte laisse alors le composant écraser
  localement la valeur. Pour bloquer, il faut la liaison à fonctions.

  Ma recommandation : garder ce modèle. Il est idiomatique, testé (le scénario «
  refus » a son test), et documenté dans la page API et les conventions du
  plugin.

  Est-ce que ça te va ? Si oui, je lance la vague 1 des composants.

       9  ## Shape
      10  
      11  - A `.svelte` component when the component owns every element i
          t needs.
      12 -- An **action** (`use:x`) — or an attachment (`{@attach x()}`) 
         -when the behavior must react to                                
      13 -  props of the consumer's element — when the primary behavior a
         -ttaches to an element the consumer                             
      14 -  already owns. This is the counterpart of an Angular directive
         -: the action receives the host                                 
      15 -  `HTMLElement` itself and returns `update`/`destroy`. A plain 
         -function or store fits behavior with                           
      16 -  no host. Choosing a shape that differs from React requires an
         - accepted `FORM-*` finding.                                    
      12 +- An **attachment** (`{@attach x(() => options)}`) when the pri
         +mary behavior attaches to an                                   
      13 +  element the consumer already owns. This is the counterpart of
         + an Angular directive: the                                     
      14 +  attachment receives the host `HTMLElement` itself, runs insid
         +e an effect, and may mount its own                             
      15 +  surface with `mount()`. Legacy actions (`use:x`) are not used
         +. A plain function or store fits                               
      16 +  behavior with no host. Choosing a shape that differs from Rea
         +ct requires an accepted `FORM-*`                               
      17 +  finding; an accepted Angular directive decision carries over.
      18  - A Svelte component has **no host element**. The template's ro
          ot element is the semantic element;
      19    anything React puts on its root element goes there.
      20  
     ...

      59  
      60  - File `Xxx.svelte`, contract `xxx.types.ts`, spec `xxx.spec.ts
          ` (`xxx.spec.svelte.ts` when it
      60 -  uses runes), fixture `xxx.fixture.svelte`, action `xxx.action
         -.ts`, barrel                                                   
      61 -  `packages/ui-svelte/src/index.ts`.                           
      61 +  uses runes), fixture `xxx.fixture.svelte`, attachment `xxx.at
         +tachment.svelte.ts` exporting                                  
      62 +  `xxx(options: () => SvelteXxxProps)`, barrel `packages/ui-sve
         +lte/src/index.ts`.                                             
      63 +- An attachment reads its options through a getter and keeps it
         +s trigger listeners and mounted                                
      64 +  surface across option changes (child `$effect`s, `untrack` at
         + setup); defaults it applies in                                
      65 +  code are documented with `@default` on the options interface,
         + which is where docgen reads them.                             
      66  - Example stems `xxx-<case>.svelte` under `apps/doc/src/example
          s/svelte/`.

      19  - Content goes through snippets: `children` for the default slo
          t, named snippets (`leadingIcon`, …) for the others — `{#snippe
          t leadingIcon()}…{/snippet}` inside the component tag. Legacy `
          <slot>` / `slot="…"` syntax is not supported.
      20  - Native attributes and DOM handlers (`aria-*`, `data-*`, `oncl
          ick`, `onkeydown`) are forwarded to the component's root elemen
          t through rest props. Use Svelte 5's lowercased handler form fo
          r those (`onclick`), and the camelCase callback form for the co
          mponent's own callbacks (`onPressedChange`).
      21  - To reach the rendered element, use `bind:this` on the compone
          nt only where its props type declares a bindable element ref; o
